HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_29fe5986b9c4287f331b","text":"88:5-7 made the wage reading newly visible by supplying a complete but useless ration after the labor. Then 88:8's opposite facial condition corrected `عاملة ناصبة` from a loose pair of traits into one side of an outcome contrast: expenditure here has no beneficial return.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000318/B001","root_001046/B003","root_001507/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4a853a02f432dda360c0","text":"2. **Officially assigned labor under later accounting.** Evidence: `ع م ل` B003 official charge, B004 wage/pay; `ن ص ب` B005 assigned share; later `ح س ب` B001 accounting and `س ع ي` B002 endeavor. Structural cue: the surah ends with return and account, and the positive group is pleased with its `سعي`. Role: `عاملة` can be the recordable performance of a charge; `ناصبة` can be a fixed allotment or burden. Abductive move: the final account retroactively frames activity as assessed labor. Reading change: the ayah reads as labor entered into an economy of recompense, where the share received is exhaustion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001046/B001","root_001507/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6dfb46bb0e1d9acbc836","text":"1. **Work as purposive action turned into exhausting affliction.** Evidence: `ع م ل` B001 intentional doing/work, B007 self-exertion; `ن ص ب` B004 weariness, strain, affliction. Structural cue: these participles stand between lowered faces and entering hot fire. Role: work supplies agency, while nasab converts it into fatigue with no saving result. Abductive move: intentional action can become punishment when its direction and yield are disordered. Reading change: the ayah reads as failed effort embodied as weariness, not just “working hard.”","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001046/B004","root_001507/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9be2d950b362782ed909","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:3:1\",\"root_001046\",[\"B004\"]],[\"88:3:2\",\"root_001507\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Ücret ve ayrılmış pay çağrışımları, sonraki zararlı yiyecek-içeceği emeğin karşılığı gibi gösterir; bu düzende yorgunluk hem süreç hem de tek kazançtır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001046/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_a10d4dd68863a573cd2f","text":"2. **The absent wage turns the scene into a failed labor economy.** **Lexical:** `ع م ل` B004, \"wage or pay for work,\" activated behind the surface worker image, together with `ن ص ب` B005, \"an assigned share or portion.\" **Cue:** what follows is an assigned consumption regime: one hot source, no food except `ضريع`, and no bodily or hunger-relieving return in 88:5-7; 88:8 then opens the opposite facial condition. **Role:** the wage branch poses the expected return on work, while the share branch casts the ensuing provisions as the worker's allotment. **Abduction:** I infer that sequential provision is compensation or outcome; that economic arrow is not grammatically stated. **Reading change:** `عاملة ناصبة` becomes labor whose allotted return compounds loss, making exhaustion both the work's process and its only \"payment.\"","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_aa6f2278ab21ec327eac","text":"The later `الجبال كيف نصبت` changed this ayah most strongly: `ناصبة` no longer reads only as tiredness but as a human body set upright under load, a punitive mirror of cosmic installation. The account ending made the labor sound recordable and payable, but with exhaustion as the wage.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001507/B001","root_001507/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_b76fdb98fa1408602540","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:3:2\",\"root_001507\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Dağların da sağlamca dikilmesi, buradaki bedeni yük altında dikilmiş bir yapıya çevirir ve doğru yerleştirilmiş kozmik yapı ile tüketen insan emeğini karşı karşıya getirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001507/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d452f9d234cef6299bc3","text":"3. **A standing installation reversed into strain.** Evidence: `ن ص ب` B001 setting upright and prominent; later 88:19 `نصبت` for mountains and `ر ف ع` B001 for raised couches/sky. Structural cue: the same root later describes mountains being set up. Role: here the uprightness becomes bodily strain rather than stable cosmic emplacement. Abductive move: a form that can install mountains can, when applied to persons, install them in fatigue. Reading change: `ناصبة` carries both toil and being fixed under toil.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001046/B001","root_001046/B007","root_001507/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_f2f3541af5f2498366f9","text":"1. **Work and weariness form a closed causal pair.** **Lexical:** `ع م ل` B001, \"intentional doing and work,\" and B007, \"self-exertion or taking trouble\"; `ن ص ب` B004, \"weariness, strain, and affliction.\" **Cue:** two feminine active participles are juxtaposed without a conjunction, and the resulting sequence proceeds directly to fire, hot drink, and non-nourishing food in 88:4-7. **Role:** `عاملة` supplies purposeful expenditure, while `ناصبة` names expenditure's consuming bodily result. **Abduction:** I infer that the second participle is consequence as well as co-description; the packet supplies tight apposition but no explicit \"therefore.\" **Reading change:** the line reads as a mechanism of labor that reproduces only exhaustion rather than as two independent epithets.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001046/B001","root_001046/B007","root_001507/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f44d9732794811bc050c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"88:3:1\",\"root_001046\",[\"B001\",\"B007\"]],[\"88:3:2\",\"root_001507\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İki sıfat kapalı bir neden-sonuç düzeni kurar: Amaçlı emek yararlı bir sonuca değil, kendini yeniden üreten tükenişe dönüşür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
